# Raredar — answer bank

Generated from `data/prompts.txt` and `data/free-prompts.txt`. 256 prompts, 21832 accepted answers.

## What each tier does

| Tier | Points | What the jet does |
|---|---|---|
| Sonic Boom | 100 | Afterburner, shockwave ring, screen shake |
| Stealth | 85 | Very strong burn, vapour trail |
| Ace | 60 | Strong burn |
| Wingman | 30 | Steady acceleration |
| Showboat | 15 | Small thrust; the tower is unimpressed |
| Rookie | 10 | Small puff of thrust |
| Stall | 0 | Engine sputters, jet wobbles (only when time runs out — wrong answers can be retried) |

Two Ace-or-better answers in a row light the afterburner; from the third in a row each answer gets +10.
Matching ignores capitals, accents, punctuation and a leading "the"; one typo is allowed in 5–8 letter answers, two in 9+.
